Strategy rules (updated Nov 18, 2026)

From Nov 18, 2026: pools are ranked on their 30-day mean APY from DefiLlama, not today's rate. A pool whose current APY is more than 1.5× its 30-day mean is skipped as a target. If a held pool is in that state, its yield is counted at the 30-day mean when deciding whether a move pays back. A target's yield for payback is the lower of its live rate and its 30-day mean. Rebalances still need +1.5 pp and must pay back simulated move costs within 21 days. Gas and cost modelling are unchanged.

Effective APY uses organic apyBase plus a 25% haircut on reward APY. This is a higher-risk book targeting about 12% blended APY. Simulated gas is deducted (about $0.50 on L2s, $3.00 on Ethereum). Entry/move costs currently sitting in open positions: $27.00. Max 30% per pool. TVL floor $5M (prefer $20M+). Rebalance if a pool beats a sleeve by 1.5 pp and move costs break even within 21 days.
